    The decline came on a combination of an oversupplied
North Sea crude market and a retreating forward market after                         Latest S&P Global Commodities at Sea data shows 15.2 million

$98.485/b, Platts data showed.                                                   the pre-war level.

    The Dated Brent physical differential, which reflects the most
competitive differential among the grades in the basket for each
day of the assessment, also sank $2.13/b on the day to a discount
of $2.885/b against the North Sea Dated strip Nov. 4. This was its
lowest value since May 2020.

    The decline in outright prices tracked a slide in the forward
market through the session, with futures prices retreating as
market participants assessed the impact of the US midterm
elections, which saw Democrats taking control of both houses of
Congress.

    Platts assessed the front-month January ICE Brent futures
contract at $98.53/b at the London close Nov. 4, down $5.07/b or
4.89% from its previous settlement.

    "The flat price has already shown the [bearish] sentiment,"
said a Europe-based North Sea crude trader, commenting on the
pricing impact of the elections.

    Analysts noted that the election results and diplomatic
progress in the Middle East have both led to an unwinding of
some geopolitical risk premium in the crude futures market.

    "The market increasingly appears to be betting that Trump
may seek to put the Middle East conflict behind him following the
election setback," said Ole Hansen, Head of Commodity Strategy
at Saxo.

    "Recent developments have encouraged hopes of a
diplomatic breakthrough, although significant differences remain
between Washington and Tehran," said Hansen.

    "The new Congress could use its influence over spending to
challenge additional military funding and increase pressure for

                                                                       operations in December after completing an initial five-well
                                                                       campaign at the Loma Jarillosa Este block earlier this year.

                                                                           The company has incorporated technical information from
                                                                       those wells into its development plans and recently completed a
                                                                       3D seismic survey to support future drilling, Bayón said.

                                                                           "Vaca Muerta is going to be a highly intensive capex
                                                                       development over the next few years," Bayón said.

                                                                           GeoPark has also completed initial connections to regional oil
                                                                       and natural gas pipelines, reducing its reliance on trucking and
                                                                       improving its ability to handle additional production.

    "A Democratic sweep changes the political cost of continuing
the Iran war," Rystad Energy Chief Economist Claudio Galimberti
said in a Nov. 4 note. "With both chambers now capable of
passing resolutions, holding back war funding and delaying
confirmations, the administration faces a shorter political runway
and greater institutional pressure to find a Hormuz deal."

    Despite easing sentiment over US-Iran relations, Russia-Ukraine
hostilities pose a persistent upside risk to the diesel market.

    "The massive toll taken on Russian refining by Ukrainian drone
strikes over the past few days is bullish for global diesel cracks,"
said S&P Global Energy CERA analyst James Bambino. "We
estimate nearly 47% of Russia's more than 7 million b/d of crude
and condensate refining is now offline, with nearly 17%-19% taken
offline in the last few days alone."

Democratic Senate complicates Trump nominations
    Senate Democrats are set to gain new powers to constrain

Trump's personnel picks, including cabinet-level nominees,
federal judges and nominees to independent agencies.

    The shift could carry significant implications for federal energy
policy. Senate Democrats are poised to control the confirmation
process for nominees to agencies, including the Federal Energy
Regulatory Commission, where turnover in the next Congress
could affect federal policy on electricity markets, transmission
and natural gas infrastructure.

    A Democratic Senate could also complicate any potential
effort by Trump to fill a Supreme Court vacancy. The court's 6-3
conservative majority, featuring three Trump nominees, has already
curtailed judicial deference to federal agencies and expanded
presidential control over traditionally independent agencies.

    Democratic control of both chambers would put a premium on
bipartisan dealmaking for legislation capable of clearing Congress
and earning Trump's signature. Congressional observers still see

    During the Edmonton turnaround on the gasoline-making fluid
catalytic cracking unit, rather than reduce crude runs of between
45,000 -50,000 b/d, Suncor was able to store the heavier gas oil
and blend it with the upgrader streams from its Fort McMurray oil
production to create a new, sweet synthetic crude.

    "This innovative idea enabled full crude throughput during the
entire turnaround, preserving 46,000 b/d of throughput would
have been curtailed, adding 3% to Edmonton's annual average
utilization rate and 1% to Suncor's overall utilization rate," said
Kruger, adding it created C$50 million in free funds.

    Suncor said this raised custom-blend processing by 8,000 b/d
this year, worth C$75 million with the added diesel yield bringing
Edmonton's year-to-date gains to C$140 million.

    In the US, Cenovus' larger refining presence reported Q3
crude throughput of 317,000 b/d, or about 87% utilization due
to the turnaround at its Lima, Ohio, refinery which ran from
early September to end October. Despite high margins, Cenovus
elected to not defer maintenance until 2027 as some of its
peers have done. Regardless, market capture rose to 88% in Q3
compared to 67% in Q2.

    Cenovus' US downstream operates in the Midwestern US, and
includes a 44,000 b/d Superior, Wisconsin, refinery, which runs all
Cenovus crude, and two Ohio refineries - Lima and Toledo.

    McKenzie said part of the higher market capture was the
ability to move refined product out of the Midwest into the US
Atlantic Coast on recently expanded capacity of the Laurel
Pipe Line, which stretches across Pennsylvania connecting the
Midwest with the US Atlantic Coast.

                                                                      delivered into North Asia at 4:30 pm Singapore time (0830 GMT).
